ASSET_REQUEST
type: weapon_icon
id: arma_EFE_rifle
filename: arma_EFE_rifle.png
size: 600x400 (o más grande con la misma proporción)
style: Hearts of Iron IV style, retrofuturist year 2100, painterly propaganda-poster look, strong silhouette readable at small size, no text, no letters, no watermark; eco-fascist green empire of South America: jungle, living bio-steel, trees and laurels, green and gold, imperial and organic
description: WHAT: the faction's standard infantry rifle, side view - the Thorn Rifle (Fusil Espina). EXACT DESIGN: A long organic rifle that looks grown from a single thorny branch: the stock is polished bark-brown wood with visible growth rings, the barrel is a straight chitin spine covered in short sharp thorns along the top, and the projectiles are hardened thorns fed from a bulbous seed-pod magazine under the grip that pulses faintly amber. A living vine wraps the fore-grip like a sling, two small leaves act as the rear sight, and a single gold laurel inlay sits on the stock. No metal receiver, no screws: it is a weapon-plant. FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - GROWN, NOT BUILT. The Ecofascist Empire does not manufacture weapons, it cultivates them in bio-forges: every object is partly alive, made of "bio-steel", a dark green-bronze material that looks like lacquered hardwood fused with insect chitin, with visible growth rings, veins and knots. No straight lines, no bolts, no welds: surfaces flow like roots and shells, joints are sinew-like tendons, cooling is done by living leaves or gills, and glowing amber sap runs in translucent veins. Metal appears only as small gold imperial laurel inlays. Silhouettes are animal-like (the weapons are named after South American fauna and really resemble them). Palette: deep forest green, bark brown, chitin bronze, amber sap glow, small gold accents. MUST BE UNMISTAKABLE: a player has to recognise the faction from the silhouette alone, without colour. Do NOT draw a generic modern weapon with a different paint job; change the shape, the materials, the mechanism and the proportions. For contrast, other factions build the same weapon like this (do NOT look like them): ASC: Not a rifle carried by a soldier: a compact robotic rifle-arm module with no stock, no tri...; FCU: A premium smart rifle sold like a luxury product: sleek bullpup body in glossy navy carbon...; HSN: A marine's rifle built from ship hardware: brass fittings, a barrel wrapped in tarred rope...; NAS: A long, light solar rifle: a slim golden alloy barrel with a fan of small golden solar pet...; SHD: A simple, standardized mass-produced rifle: a straight boxy stamped-steel body, identical ...; APF: A rugged, simple rifle meant to be repaired in a village: an AK-like reliable shape update...; NRE: A heavy, ceremonial legionary rifle shaped like a pilum: an unusually long slim barrel tha...; ZWE: A Soviet-era bolt-action or Kalashnikov-type rifle dug out of a depot and rebuilt in a ste...; ZWI: An old rifle turned into a festival object: brass inlays hammered into the wooden stock in...; ZWM: An expensive imported smart rifle with gold-plated parts, a jambiya-curved dagger as bayon...; ZWB: A prospector's rifle: an old hunting rifle whose wooden stock is caked in dried red mud, t...; ZAN: A frontier rifle assembled from plumbing pipes and scrap: a galvanised pipe barrel, an elb... Rusty-brown and green, sleek and alert. FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - GROWN, NOT BUILT. The Ecofascist Empire does not manufacture weapons, it cultivates them in bio-forges: every object is partly alive, made of "bio-steel", a dark green-bronze material that looks like lacquered hardwood fused with insect chitin, with visible growth rings, veins and knots. No straight lines, no bolts, no welds: surfaces flow like roots and shells, joints are sinew-like tendons, cooling is done by living leaves or gills, and glowing amber sap runs in translucent veins. Metal appears only as small gold imperial laurel inlays. Silhouettes are animal-like (the weapons are named after South American fauna and really resemble them). Palette: deep forest green, bark brown, chitin bronze, amber sap glow, small gold accents. MUST BE UNMISTAKABLE: a player has to recognise the faction from the silhouette alone, without colour. Do NOT draw a generic modern weapon with a different paint job; change the shape, the materials, the mechanism and the proportions. Clinical and cold. FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - NO HUMANS NEEDED. The Automated Socialist Commune builds crewless machines designed by planning algorithms: everything is a robot or a drone, with no seats, no hatches, no windows, no handles for human hands. White ceramic armour with red structural frames and black sensor glass, like a clean factory robot that went to war. Multiple legs or tracks are common; nothing looks handcrafted, everything looks printed in a series of thousands. Nautical details everywhere: portholes, cleats, coiled ropes, anchors, signal flags without letters, compass roses, brass fittings, container corner castings. Palette: petrol teal, rust orange, container blue and red, brass, white hull numbers as shapes. MUST BE UNMISTAKABLE: a player has to recognise the faction from the silhouette alone, without colour. Do NOT draw a generic modern weapon with a different paint job; change the shape, the materials, the mechanism and the proportions. For contrast, other factions build the same weapon like this (do NOT look like them): EFE: A slender anti-aircraft emitter shaped like a hummingbird's beak and head on a tall flower...; ASC: An air-defence robot like a tall white tripod tower with a rotating red geometric head mad...; FCU: A sleek air-defence system shaped like a sports-car on a turntable: curved chrome housing ...; NAS: An air-defence device shaped like the Intihuatana sun-stone: a carved stone pillar with a ...; SHD: An anti-aircraft battery of four identical boxy laser emitters on a square hydraulic mount...; APF: An air-defence laser on the back of a rugged pickup: a solar-panel canopy, big off-road ty...; NRE: An air-defence laser shaped like a giant aquila legion standard: a crimson-and-gold pole a...; ZWE: A towed twin-barrel anti-aircraft gun from an old depot, rebuilt with mismatched barrels o...; ZWI: An anti-aircraft gun under a small temple-like canopy with a curved dome roof, a golden ga...; ZWM: An air-defence vehicle shaped like a hunting falcon with spread wings: two large curved ra...; ZWB: An anti-aircraft gun mounted on a rusty river barge with a carved harpy eagle head on the ...; ZAN: A rooftop flak gun: four different machine guns welded together on top of an old satellite... Woven textile patterns (aguayo stripes) decorate armour, turquoise crystal lenses replace glass, and many weapons use gravity or wind (gliders, slings, rails). Palette: gold, turquoise, terracotta red, alpaca white and stone grey. MUST BE UNMISTAKABLE: a player has to recognise the faction from the silhouette alone, without colour. Do NOT draw a generic modern weapon with a different paint job; change the shape, the materials, the mechanism and the proportions. description: WHAT: a wheeled troop transport vehicle - the Yunhe Carrier (Transporte Yunhe). EXACT DESIGN: A standardized troop truck: a boxy jade-green cab, a canvas-covered cargo bed with benches, big simple wheels, a water tank on the side; plain and mass-produced. FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - HYDRAULIC MASS PRODUCTION. The Sino-Harmonious Directorate designs simple, standardized, rugged machines that can be produced by the million: boxy, symmetrical, heavy, with no decoration except regulation markings. Everything is water-cooled and hydraulically driven: thick pipes, pistons, radiators, pressure gauges, water tanks and dam-like sluice armour plates; heavy weapons use water-pressure or steam-assisted launchers. The look is orderly and identical, like a parade of equal units: concrete-grey and jade-green paint, stencilled geometric unit codes (no letters), small red star-like harmony emblems (a circle with flowing waves). MUST BE UNMISTAKABLE: a player has to recognise the faction from the silhouette alone, without colour. Do NOT draw a generic modern weapon with a different paint job; change the shape, the materials, the mechanism and the proportions. Everything is water-cooled and hydraulically driven: thick pipes, pistons, radiators, pressure gauges, water tanks and dam-like sluice armour plates; heavy weapons use water-pressure or steam-assisted launchers. The look is orderly and identical, like a parade of equal units: concrete-grey and jade-green paint, stencilled geometric unit codes (no letters), small red star-like harmony emblems (a circle with flowing waves). MUST BE UNMISTAKABLE: a player has to recognise the faction from the silhouette alone, without colour. Do NOT draw a generic modern weapon with a different paint job; change the shape, the materials, the mechanism and the proportions. For contrast, other factions build the same weapon like this (do NOT look like them): EFE: A huge battleship shaped like a living crown of trees: the hull is dark bronze chitin with...; ASC: A massive automated battleship: a huge geometric hull made of stacked rectangular blocks l...; FCU: A battleship as corporate flagship: enormous navy hull with chrome superstructure shaped l...; HSN: A huge capital ship converted from an ultra-large container ship: kilometre-long hull with...; NAS: A battleship as a floating sun temple: an immense hull crowned with a golden Coricancha te...; APF: The Olokun battleship (named for the Yoruba sea deity): an immense deep-blue hull with flo...; NRE: The Augustus battleship: an immense hull whose superstructure is a white marble temple wit... MUST BE UNMISTAKABLE: a player has to recognise the faction from the silhouette alone, without colour. Do NOT draw a generic modern weapon with a different paint job; change the shape, the materials, the mechanism and the proportions. description: WHAT: an amphibious armoured vehicle coming out of the water - the Pirate Raft (Balsa Pirata). FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - SCRAPYARD MONSTERS. The lawless lands build weapons from pure junk: car doors, washing machines, road signs (with no letters), shopping carts, pipes, chains and spikes, everything welded randomly, leaking, smoking and patched; skulls and raider tags painted without letters. Palette: rust, soot black, hazard yellow, faded mismatched colours. MUST BE UNMISTAKABLE: a player has to recognise the faction from the silhouette alone, without colour.
